- **relay**: PostToolUse, PreCompact, Stop, UserPromptSubmit. Relay: turn-boundary token-budget check (UserPromptSubmit), throttled mid-task emergency check (PostToolUse), 5-hour plan-usage check (Stop, reads rate_limits from the payload), and a compaction backstop (PreCompact).
